But there was only one patient waiting, and she saluted me as I entered with a snort of defiance.

===Chinese===
一路的冥想引着我绕了个远路，迟了十分钟，才到菲特巷巷口；到了那里，我把那副有些恍惚的神气换作忙碌开业医的干练风度，迈开大步匆匆向前，眉头紧锁地一头扎进诊所，仿佛刚从一桩焦心的病症上脱身出来。可是候诊的病人只有一位，我一进门，她就挑衅似的哼了一声，算是向我致意。

===Original===
“Here you are, then?” said she.

===Chinese===
“哟，您可算来了？”她说。

===Original===
“You are perfectly correct, Miss Oman,” I replied; “in fact, you have put the case in a nutshell. What can I have the pleasure of doing for you?”

===Chinese===
“您说得一点不错，奥曼小姐，”我答道，“说实在的，您一句话就把情形概括尽了。我能有幸为您效点什么劳呢？”

===Original===
“Nothing,” was the answer. “My medical adviser is a lady; but I’ve brought a note from Mr. Bellingham. Here it is,” and she thrust the envelope into my hand.

===Chinese===
“什么都不消，”回答是这么一句，“给我看病的大夫是位女士；不过我带了一张贝林厄姆先生的便条来。喏，在这儿。”说着她把信封塞进我手里。

===Original===
I glanced through the note and learned that my patient had had a couple of bad nights and a very harassing day. “Could I have something to give me a night’s rest?” it concluded.

===Chinese===
我把便条草草看了一遍，得知我的病人接连熬了两夜坏觉，又过了一天焦心的日子。便条末尾写道：“能不能给我点药，让我夜里安睡一觉？”

===Original===
I reflected for a few moments. One is not very ready to prescribe sleeping draughts for unknown patients, but still, insomnia is a very distressing condition. In the end I temporized with a moderate dose of bromide, deciding to call and see if more energetic measures were necessary.

===Chinese===
我思忖了片刻。给不相识的病人开催眠药水，谁也不会很痛快；可是失眠终究是极磨人的苦处。末了我折中办理，开了一剂适度的溴化物，决定登门探视，看是否需要更积极的处置。

===Original===
“He had better take a dose of this at once, Miss Oman,” said I, as I handed her the bottle, “and I will look in later and see how he is.”

===Chinese===
“这药最好让他立刻服一剂，奥曼小姐。”我把药瓶交给她时说，“回头我再顺道进来瞧瞧他情况如何。”

===Original===
“I expect he will be glad to see you,” she answered, “for he is all alone tonight and very dumpy. Miss Bellingham is out. But I must remind you that he’s a poor man and pays his way. You must excuse my mentioning it.”

===Chinese===
“我料想他会高兴见您的，”她答道，“因为今晚只剩他独自一人，闷闷不乐。贝林厄姆小姐出去了。不过我得提醒您一句，他是个穷人，看病的账是照付不误的。我提这个，您得多多包涵。”

===Original===
“I am much obliged to you for the hint, Miss Oman,” I rejoined. “It isn’t necessary for me to see him, but I should like just to look in and have a chat.”

===Chinese===
“多谢您提醒，奥曼小姐，”我应道，“我并非非见他不可，不过我愿意顺道进去坐一坐，聊几句。”

===Original===
“Yes, it will do him good. You have your points, though punctuality doesn’t seem to be one of them,” and with this parting shot Miss Oman bustled away.

===Chinese===
“是啊，这对他有好处。您自有您的长处，只是守时似乎不在其列。”奥曼小姐丢下这句临别的抢白，便风风火火地去了。

===Original===
Half-past eight found me ascending the great, dim staircase of the house in Nevill’s Court preceded by Miss Oman, by whom I was ushered into the room. Mr. Bellingham, who had just finished some sort of meal, was sitting hunched up in his chair gazing gloomily into the empty grate. He brightened up as I entered, but was evidently in very low spirits.

===Chinese===
八点半，我已跟着奥曼小姐登上内维尔巷那所宅子昏暗的大楼梯，由她把我引进屋里。贝林厄姆先生刚吃过一顿什么饭，蜷缩在椅子里，忧郁地凝望着空空的炉膛。我一进门他便神色一振，可情绪显然十分低落。

===Original===
“I didn’t mean to drag you out after your day’s work was finished,” he said, “though I am very glad to see you.”

===Chinese===
“我本无意在您一天的诊务完毕之后还把您拖出门，”他说，“不过见到您，我实在高兴。”

===Original===
“You haven’t dragged me out. I heard you were alone, so I just dropped in for a few minutes’ gossip.”

===Chinese===
“您并没有拖我出门。我听说您独自一人，便索性进来闲扯几分钟。”

===Original===
“That is really kind of you,” he said heartily. “But I’m afraid you’ll find me rather poor company. A man who is full of his own highly disagreeable affairs is not a desirable companion.”

===Chinese===
“您真是太好心了，”他诚挚地说，“不过恐怕您会觉得跟我做伴很乏味。一个满脑子都是自己那些大不痛快的事的人，可不是个称心的同伴。”

===Original===
“You mustn’t let me disturb you if you’d rather be alone,” said I, with a sudden fear that I was intruding.

===Chinese===
“您要是宁可一个人待着，千万别让我搅扰您。”我说，忽然担心自己是在打扰。

===Original===
“Oh, you won’t disturb me,” he replied; adding, with a laugh: “It’s more likely to be the other way about. In fact, if I were not afraid of boring you to death I would ask you to let me talk my difficulties over with you.”

===Chinese===
“哦，您不会搅扰我的，”他答道，又笑着添了一句，“倒更可能是您被我搅了。说真的，要不是怕把您腻烦死，我就要请您容我把我的难处细细谈一谈了。”

===Original===
“You won’t bore me,” I said. “It is generally interesting to share another man’s experiences without their inconveniences. ‘The proper study of mankind is﻿—man,’ you know, especially to a doctor.”

===Chinese===
“您不会腻烦我的，”我说，“不必身受其累，却能分尝他人的阅历，通常总是有趣的。您知道，‘人类最恰当的研究对象便是——人’，对医生尤其如此。”

===Original===
Mr. Bellingham chuckled grimly. “You make me feel like a microbe,” he said. “However, if you would care to take a peep at me through your microscope, I will crawl on to the stage for your inspection, though it is not *my* actions that furnish the materials for your psychological studies. It is my poor brother who is the *Deus ex machina*, who, from his unknown grave, as I fear, pulls the strings of this infernal puppet-show.”

===Chinese===
贝林厄姆先生苦笑了一声。“您让我觉得自己像只微生物，”他说，“不过，您既然肯透过显微镜窥我一眼，我就自己爬上载物台听凭检验吧——只是为您的心理研究供给材料的，并不是*我*的行止。那*Deus ex machina*（解围之神）的角色，是由我那可怜的哥哥充当的——他，我恐怕，正从那座无人知晓的坟墓里，牵动着这出可恶傀儡戏的提线。”

===Original===
He paused and for a space gazed thoughtfully into the grate as if he had forgotten my presence. At length he looked up and resumed:

===Chinese===
他顿了一顿，有一阵子只管若有所思地凝望炉膛，仿佛把我忘在了脑后。终于他抬起头来，接着说道：

===Original===
“It is a curious story, Doctor﻿—a very curious story. Part of it you know﻿—the middle part. I will tell you it from the beginning, and then you will know as much as I do; for, as to the end, that is known to no one. It is written, no doubt, in the book of destiny, but the page has yet to be turned.

===Chinese===
“这是个离奇的故事，医生——一个非常离奇的故事。其中一段您已经知道了——中间那一段。我这就从头讲起，讲完您知道的就同我一样多了；至于结局，那可是谁都还不知道。它无疑早已写在天命簿上，只是那一页还没有翻开来。

===Original===
“The mischief began with my father’s death. He was a country clergyman of very moderate means, a widower with two children, my brother John and me. He managed to send us both to Oxford, after which John went into the Foreign Office and I was to have gone into the Church. But I suddenly discovered that my views on religion had undergone a change that made this impossible, and just about this time my father came into a quite considerable property. Now, as it was his expressed intention to leave the estate equally divided between my brother and me, there was no need for me to take up any profession for a livelihood. Archeology was already the passion of my life, and I determined to devote myself henceforth to my favorite study, in which, by the way, I was following a family tendency; for my father was an enthusiastic student of ancient Oriental history, and John was, as you know, an ardent Egyptologist.

===Chinese===
“祸端起自我父亲的去世。他是个家计十分有限的乡村牧师，丧妻，带着两个孩子——我哥哥约翰和我。他设法供我们俩都进了牛津，此后约翰入了外交部，我则本要进教会任圣职。可我突然发觉自己的宗教见解起了变化，这条路走不成了；也差不多正在这时，我父亲得了一笔相当可观的产业。既然他明确表示要把产业在我和哥哥之间均分，我便无须再操什么职业谋生。考古学早已是我毕生的酷爱，于是我决意从此潜心于这门心爱的学问——顺带一说，我这也是承了家族的禀性：我父亲热心钻研古代东方史，而约翰，如您所知，是位热忱的埃及学家。

===Original===
“Then my father died quite suddenly, and left no will. He had intended to have one drawn up, but had put it off until it was too late. And since nearly all the property was in the form of real estate, my brother inherited practically the whole of it. However, in deference to the known wishes of my father, he made me an allowance of five hundred a year, which was about a quarter of the annual income. I urged him to assign me a lump sum, but he refused to do this. Instead, he instructed his solicitor to pay me an allowance in quarterly instalments during the rest of his life; and it was understood that, on his death, the entire estate should devolve on me, or if I died first, on my daughter, Ruth. Then, as you know, he disappeared suddenly, and as the circumstances suggested that he was dead, and there was no evidence that he was alive, his solicitor﻿—a Mr. Jellicoe﻿—found himself unable to continue the payment of the allowance. On the other hand, as there was no positive evidence that my brother was dead, it was impossible to administer the will.”

===Chinese===
“后来我父亲猝然去世，没有留下遗嘱。他本打算请人拟一份，却一拖再拖，拖到来不及了。由于财产几乎全是不动产，我哥哥实际上继承了全部。不过，为遵从我父亲素来明示的心愿，他给了我每年五百镑的津贴，约合年收入的四分之一。我曾力劝他拨给我一笔整款，他不肯。他只吩咐他的律师在他在世之日按季付我津贴；并且讲定，他一去世，全部产业即转由我继承；倘我先死，则归我女儿露丝。再后来，如您所知，他突然失踪了；种种情形表明他已死亡，又没有他活着的任何证据，他的律师——一位杰利科先生——便无法继续支付津贴了。可另一方面，又没有我哥哥确已死亡的明确证据，遗嘱也就无从执行。”

===Original===
“You say the circumstances suggested that your brother was dead. What circumstances were they?”

===Chinese===
“您说种种情形表明您哥哥已经死了。都是些什么情形？”

===Original===
“Principally the suddenness and completeness of the disappearance. His luggage, as you may remember, was found lying unclaimed at the railway station; and there was another circumstance even more suggestive. My brother drew a pension from the Foreign Office, for which he had to apply in person, or, if abroad, produce proof that he was alive on the date when the payment became due. Now, he was exceedingly regular in this respect; in fact, he had never been known to fail, either to appear in person or to transmit the necessary documents to his agent, Mr. Jellicoe. But from the moment when he vanished so mysteriously to the present day, nothing whatever has been heard of him.”

===Chinese===
“主要是失踪得突然而彻底。您也许记得，他的行李一直无人认领，扔在火车站里；还有一桩情形更能发人深思。我哥哥从外交部领一份养老金，这钱须他本人亲自去领；他若身在国外，则须出具有关付款到期之日他尚在人世的证明。要知道，他在这件事上一向极守规矩；事实上，从不曾见他误过一回——不是亲自出面，就是把必要的文件寄给他的代理人杰利科先生。可自他神秘失踪的那一刻起，直到今天，关于他，再没有半点音信。”

===Original===
“It’s a very awkward position for you,” I said, “but I should think there will not be much difficulty in obtaining the permission of the Court to presume death and to proceed to prove the will.”

===Chinese===
“这处境对您实在尴尬，”我说，“不过我想，要取得法院的许可、推定死亡，进而办理遗嘱认证，该不会有多大困难。”

===Original===
Mr. Bellingham made a wry face. “I expect you are right,” he said, “but that doesn’t help me much. You see, Mr. Jellicoe, having waited a reasonable time for my brother to reappear, took a very unusual but, I think, in the special circumstances, a very proper step; he summoned me and the other interested party to his office and communicated to us the provisions of the will. And very extraordinary provisions they turned out to be. I was thunderstruck when I heard them. And the exasperating thing is that I feel sure my poor brother imagined that he had made everything perfectly safe and simple.”

===Chinese===
贝林厄姆先生做了个苦脸。“我想您说得对，”他说，“可那帮不了我多少忙。您瞧，杰利科先生等我哥哥重新露面，等了相当时日之后，采取了一个极不寻常、然而我认为在特殊情况之下却十分得体的步骤：他把我和另一位利害当事人召到他的事务所，把遗嘱的条款向我们宣示了。那些条款竟是非常离奇。我一听简直如遭雷击。而最叫人恼火的是，我敢肯定，我那可怜的哥哥还自以为他把一切安排得万稳万妥、简单明了呢。”

===Original===
“They generally do,” I said, rather vaguely.

===Chinese===
“立遗嘱的人大都这么想。”我含含糊糊地说。

===Original===
“I suppose they do,” said Mr. Bellingham; “but poor John has made the most infernal hash of his will, and I am certain that he has utterly defeated his own intentions. You see, we are an old London family. The house in Queen Square where my brother nominally lived, but actually kept his collection, has been occupied by us for generations, and most of the Bellinghams are buried in St. George’s burial-ground close by, though some members of the family are buried in other churchyards in the neighborhood. Now, my brother﻿—who, by the way, was a bachelor﻿—had a strong feeling for the family traditions, and he stipulated, not unnaturally, in his will that he should be buried in St. George’s burial-ground among his ancestors, or, at least, in one of the places of burial appertaining to his native parish. But instead of simply expressing the wish and directing his executors to carry it out, he made it a condition affecting the operation of the will.”

===Chinese===
“我想是吧，”贝林厄姆先生说，“可可怜的约翰把他的遗嘱弄得一塌糊涂，糟到无以复加，我敢说他把自己的本意整个儿断送了。您知道，我们是伦敦的老户人家。皇后广场（Queen Square）那所宅子——我哥哥名义上住在那里，实际上存放着他的收藏——我们家已经住了好几代，贝林厄姆家的大多数人都葬在近旁的圣乔治墓园（St. George's burial-ground），虽然家族中也有几位葬在左近别的教堂墓地。喏，我哥哥——顺带一说，他终身未娶——对家族的传统极看重，于是他在遗嘱里订明，身后须葬入圣乔治墓园、陪伴列祖列宗，或者至少葬在隶属他本教区的墓地之一——这倒也不算悖理。可他不单是把这心愿说出、嘱托执行人照办，而是把它立成了牵动遗嘱效力的一个条件。”

===Original===
“Affecting it in what respect?” I asked.

===Chinese===
“牵动到哪个方面？”我问。

===Original===
“In a very vital respect,” answered Mr. Bellingham. “The bulk of the property he bequeathed to me, or if I predeceased him, to my daughter Ruth. But the bequest was subject to the condition I have mentioned﻿—that he should be buried in a certain place﻿—and if that condition was not fulfilled, the bulk of the property was to go to my cousin, George Hurst.”

===Chinese===
“牵动到一个生死攸关的方面，”贝林厄姆先生答道，“他把财产的主体遗留给我；倘若我先他而死，则遗给我女儿露丝。但这笔遗赠附着我方才说的那个条件——他必须安葬在一定的地方——条件若不履行，财产的主体便归我的表亲乔治·赫斯特（George Hurst）所有。”

===Original===
“But in that case,” said I, “as you can’t produce the body, neither of you can get the property.”

===Chinese===
“可在那种情形下，”我说，“既然您拿不出遗体，你们两位谁也拿不到产业。”

===Original===
“I am not so sure of that,” he replied. “If my brother is dead, it is pretty certain that he is not buried in St. George’s or any of the other places mentioned, and the fact can easily be proved by production of the registers. So that a permission to presume death would result in the handing over to Hurst of almost the entire estate.”

===Chinese===
“这我倒拿不准，”他答道，“倘若我哥哥死了，那他几乎可以断定没有葬在圣乔治或提到的任何别的墓地，这一点只要出示登记簿便很容易证明。所以，一旦获准推定死亡，结果便是把几乎全部产业交到赫斯特手里。”

===Original===
“Who is the executor?” I asked.

===Chinese===
“执行人是谁？”我问。

===Original===
“Ah!” he exclaimed, “there is another muddle. There are two executors; Jellicoe is one, and the other is the principal beneficiary﻿—Hurst or myself, as the case may be. But, you see, neither of us can become an executor until the Court has decided which of us is the principal beneficiary.”

===Chinese===
“啊！”他叫道，“这又是一笔糊涂账。执行人有两位：杰利科是一位，另一位是主要受益人——视情形而定，或是赫斯特，或是我。可您瞧，法院一日不裁定我们俩谁是主要受益人，我们俩谁也当不成执行人。”

===Original===
“But who is to apply to the Court? I thought that was the business of the executors.”

===Chinese===
“那么由谁向法院提出申请呢？我原以为那是执行人的事务。”

===Original===
“Exactly, that is Hurst’s difficulty. We were discussing it when you called the other day, and a very animated discussion it was,” he added, with a grim smile. “You see, Jellicoe naturally refuses to move in the matter alone. He says he must have the support of the other executor. But Hurst is not at present the other executor; neither am I. But the two of us together are the co-executor, since the duty devolves upon one or other of us, in any case.”

===Chinese===
“正是，这正是赫斯特的难处。前几天您来访时我们正讨论这事，那可真是一场火热的讨论。”他带着一脸苦笑补了一句，“您瞧，杰利科自然不肯独自出面办理此事。他说必须得到另一位执行人的支持。可赫斯特眼下并不是另一位执行人，我也不是。但我们两个合起来便是那另一位共同执行人，反正这份职责终归要落到我们二人中某一位的头上。”

===Original===
“It’s a complicated position,” I said.

===Chinese===
“这局面真够复杂的。”我说。

===Original===
“It is; and the complication has elicited a very curious proposal from Hurst. He points out﻿—quite correctly, I am afraid﻿—that as the conditions as to burial have not been complied with, the property must come to him, and he proposes a very neat little arrangement, which is this: That I shall support him and Jellicoe in their application for permission to presume death and to administer the will, and that he shall pay me four hundred a year for life; the arrangement to hold good *in all eventualities*.”

===Chinese===
“可不是；而这层复杂又引出了赫斯特一个非常古怪的提议。他指出——恐怕说得相当对——安葬的条件既未履行，产业必归他无疑，于是他提出一个非常精巧的小安排，内容是：由我支持他和杰利科申请推定死亡的许可并执行遗嘱，而他则付给我每年四百镑，以终身为限；此项安排*无论出现何种结局*一律有效。”

===Original===
“What does he mean by that?”

===Chinese===
“他这话是什么意思？”

===Original===
“He means,” said Bellingham, fixing me with a ferocious scowl, “that if the body should turn up at any future time, so that the conditions as to burial should be able to be carried out, he should still retain the property and pay me the four hundred a year.”

===Chinese===
“他的意思是，”贝林厄姆恶狠狠地皱眉瞪着我说，“倘若遗体日后万一冒了出来，安葬的条件得以履行，他也照样保有产业，照样每年付我四百镑。”

===Original===
“The deuce!” said I. “He seems to know how to drive a bargain.”

===Chinese===
“见鬼！”我说，“讨价还价他倒是很在行。”

===Original===
“His position is that he stands to lose four hundred a year for the term of my life if the body is never found, and he ought to stand to win if it is.”

===Chinese===
“他的道理是：遗体若是永远找不回来，他就要在我有生之年一直白贴每年四百镑；那么找得回来，他也理应稳赢这一注才是。”

===Original===
“And I gather that you have refused this offer?”

===Chinese===
“那么我料想，您已经回绝了这个提议？”

===Original===
“Yes; very emphatically, and my daughter agrees with me; but I am not sure that I have done the right thing. A man should think twice, I suppose, before he burns his boats.”

===Chinese===
“回了；回绝得斩钉截铁，我女儿也同我一条心；只是我拿不准这件事做对了没有。我想，一个人在破釜沉舟之前，总该三思才是。”

===Original===
“Have you spoken to Mr. Jellicoe about the matter?”

===Chinese===
“这件事您同杰利科先生谈过么？”

===Original===
“Yes, I have been to see him today. He is a cautious man, and he doesn’t advise me one way or the other. But I think he disapproves of my refusal; in fact, he remarked that a bird in the hand is worth two in the bush, especially when the whereabouts of the bush is unknown.”

===Chinese===
“谈过，我今天去看过他。他是个谨慎人，不肯往哪一边劝我。不过我想他是并不赞成我回绝的；事实上，他说了句一鸟在手胜于二鸟在林——尤其是那片林子的下落还无人知晓。”

===Original===
“Do you think he will apply to the Court without your sanction?”

===Chinese===
“您认为没有您的首肯，他也会向法院提出申请么？”

===Original===
“He doesn’t want to; but I suppose, if Hurst puts pressure on him, he will have to. Besides, Hurst, as an interested party, could apply on his own account, and after my refusal he probably will; at least, that is Jellicoe’s opinion.”

===Chinese===
“他不愿意；可我想，赫斯特若向他施压，他也就不得不如此了。再说，赫斯特身为利害当事人，本可以自行申请；我既已回绝，他多半会走这一步——至少，杰利科是这么看的。”

===Original===
“The whole thing is a most astonishing muddle,” I said, “especially when one remembers that your brother had a lawyer to advise him. Didn’t Mr. Jellicoe point out to him how absurd the provisions were?”

===Chinese===
“这整件事真是一团惊人的乱麻，”我说，“尤其是想到您哥哥身边自有律师替他出主意。难道杰利科先生当时没向他指出这些条款有多么荒唐？”

===Original===
“Yes, he did. He tells me that he implored my brother to let him draw up a will embodying the matter in a reasonable form. But John wouldn’t listen to him. Poor old fellow! he could be very pigheaded when he chose.”

===Chinese===
“指出了。他告诉我，他曾恳求我哥哥让他按合乎情理的形式把这件事拟进遗嘱。可约翰听不进去。可怜的老家伙！他犯起倔来，可是顽固得很。”

===Original===
“And is Hurst’s proposal still open?”

===Chinese===
“那么赫斯特的提议还算数么？”

===Original===
“No, thanks to my peppery temper. I refused it very definitely, and sent him off with a flea in his ear. I hope I have not made a false step; I was quite taken by surprise when Hurst made the proposal and got rather angry. You remember, my brother was last seen alive at Hurst’s house﻿—but there, I oughtn’t to talk like that, and I oughtn’t to pester you with my confounded affairs when you come in for a friendly chat, though I gave you fair warning, you remember.”

===Chinese===
“不算了，都怪我这场火爆脾气。我回绝得一干二净，还抢白了他几句，把他轰走了。但愿我没有走错这一步；赫斯特提出那提议时，我全然措手不及，火气就上来了。您记得吧，我哥哥最后一次被人见到活着，是在赫斯特家里——罢了，我不该这样说话，也不该拿这些该死的私事来搅扰您一场好意来访——虽然我事先警告过您的，您记得。”

===Original===
“Oh, but you have been highly entertaining. You don’t realize what an interest I take in your case.”

===Chinese===
“哦，您方才真使我趣味盎然。您不知道我对您这案子怀着多大的兴味呢。”

===Original===
Mr. Bellingham laughed somewhat grimly. “My case!” he repeated. “You speak as if I were some rare and curious sort of criminal lunatic. However, I’m glad you find me amusing. It’s more than I find myself.”

===Chinese===
贝林厄姆先生笑得有几分凄冷。“我的案子！”他重复道，“您说得我仿佛是哪种珍稀而离奇的刑事疯子似的。不过，您既觉得我有味，我总该高兴——这可比我对自家的观感强多了。”

===Original===
“I didn’t say amusing; I said interesting. I view you with deep respect as the central figure of a stirring drama. And I am not the only person who regards you in that light. Do you remember my speaking to you of Doctor Thorndyke?”

===Chinese===
“我没说有味；我说的是有趣。我是怀着深深的敬意看待您的——您是一出动人心魄的戏剧的中心人物。而且如此看待您的，还不止我一人。您还记得我同您谈起过桑代克博士么？”

===Original===
“Yes, of course I do.”

===Chinese===
“记得，当然记得。”

===Original===
“Well, oddly enough, I met him this afternoon and we had a long talk at his chambers. I took the liberty of mentioning that I had made your acquaintance. Did I do wrong?”

===Chinese===
“嗳，说来也巧，今天下午我遇见了他，还在他寓所里同他长谈了一场。我冒昧提了一句，说我已同您相识。我这样做错了么？”

===Original===
“No. Certainly not. Why shouldn’t you tell him? Did he remember my infernal case, as you call it?”

===Chinese===
“不。当然没有。为什么不可以告诉他？他记得我那桩——照您的说法——该死的案子么？”

===Original===
“Perfectly, in all its details. He is quite an enthusiast, you know, and uncommonly keen to hear how the case develops.”

===Chinese===
“记得清清楚楚，连细枝末节都记得。您知道，他实在是个热心此道的人，急切得很，想听听这案子如何发展。”

===Original===
“So am I, for that matter,” said Mr. Bellingham.

===Chinese===
“要说急切，我也一样呢。”贝林厄姆先生说。

===Original===
“I wonder,” said I, “if you would mind my telling him what you have told me tonight? It would interest him enormously.”

===Chinese===
“我不知，”我说，“您介不介意让我把您今晚对我讲的转告给他？那会叫他极感兴趣的。”

===Original===
Mr. Bellingham reflected for a while with his eyes fixed on the empty grate. Presently he looked up, and said slowly:

===Chinese===
贝林厄姆先生眼睛盯着空空的炉膛，思量了好一会儿。片刻之后他抬起头来，缓缓说道：

===Original===
“I don’t know why I should. It’s no secret; and if it were, I hold no monopoly in it. No; tell him, if you think he’d care to hear about it.”

===Chinese===
“我不明白有什么可忌讳的。这不是什么秘密；就算是的，我也并非独占这秘密的人。不，您告诉他吧，只要您认为他乐意听。”

===Original===
“You needn’t be afraid of his talking,” I said. “He’s as close as an oyster; and the facts may mean more to him than they do to us. He may be able to give a useful hint or two.”

===Chinese===
“您不必担心他嘴快，”我说，“他严实得像只牡蛎；再说，这些事实在他眼里或许比在我们眼里意味着更多的东西。他说不定能提上一两条有用的提示。”

===Original===
“Oh, I’m not going to pick his brains,” Mr. Bellingham said quickly and with some wrath. “I’m not the sort of man who goes round cadging for free professional advice. Understand that, Doctor.”

===Chinese===
“哦，我可没打算去掏他的脑髓，”贝林厄姆先生急忙说道，还带着几分恼怒，“我不是那种到处打秋风、讨免费行家意见的人。这一点请您明白，医生。”

===Original===
“I do,” I answered hastily. “That wasn’t what I meant at all. Is that Miss Bellingham coming in? I heard the front door shut.”

===Chinese===
“我明白，”我赶紧答道，“我根本不是那个意思。是贝林厄姆小姐回来了么？我听见前门关上了。”

===Original===
“Yes, that will be my girl, I expect; but don’t run away. You’re not afraid of her, are you?” he added as I hurriedly picked up my hat.

===Chinese===
“是啊，我料想那准是我家姑娘；可您别跑。您总不至于怕她吧？”见我慌忙拿起帽子，他加了一句。

===Original===
“I’m not sure that I’m not,” I answered. “She is rather a majestic young lady.”

===Chinese===
“怕不怕，我还真说不准呢。”我答道，“她是位颇有威仪的年轻小姐。”

===Original===
Mr. Bellingham chuckled and smothered a yawn, and at that moment his daughter entered the room; and, in spite of her shabby black dress and a shabbier handbag that she carried, I thought her appearance and manner fully justified my description.

===Chinese===
贝林厄姆先生呵呵一笑，掩住一个呵欠；正在这时，他女儿走进了房间。尽管她一身寒素的黑衣裙，手里又拎着一只更为寒旧的手提包，我却觉得，她的仪容气度完全当得起我方才那句形容。

===Original===
“You come in, Miss Bellingham,” I said as she shook my hand with cool civility, “to find your father yawning and me taking my departure. So I have my uses, you see. My conversation is the infallible cure for insomnia.”

===Chinese===
“您来得正巧，贝林厄姆小姐，”她淡然而有礼地同我握手时，我说道，“赶上令尊打呵欠、我正要告辞。您瞧，我总算有我的用处：我的谈话是治失眠万无一失的灵药。”

===Original===
Miss Bellingham smiled. “I believe I am driving you away,” she said.

===Chinese===
贝林厄姆小姐微微一笑。“我看是我把您赶走了。”她说。

===Original===
“Not at all,” I replied hastily. “My mission was accomplished, that was all.”

===Chinese===
“决没有的事，”我急忙答道，“我的使命已经完成，仅此而已。”

===Original===
“Sit down for a few moments, Doctor,” urged Mr. Bellingham, “and let Ruth sample the remedy. She will be affronted if you run away as soon as she comes in.”

===Chinese===
“再坐几分钟吧，医生，”贝林厄姆先生劝道，“也让露丝试试这剂药。您若一见她进门就跑，她可要怪罪的。”

===Original===
“Well, you mustn’t let me keep you up,” I said.

===Chinese===
“好吧，可别让我耽误您休息。”我说。

===Original===
“Oh, I’ll let you know when I fall asleep,” he replied, with a chuckle; and with this understanding I sat down again﻿—not at all unwillingly.

===Chinese===
“哦，我一睡着自会告诉您。”他呵呵笑着答道；有了这一层谅解，我便重新坐下——心里可一点也不勉强。

===Original===
At this moment Miss Oman entered with a small tray and a smile of which I should not have supposed her capable.

===Chinese===
正在这时，奥曼小姐端着一只小托盘走了进来，脸上带着一种我原以为她断乎不会有的微笑。

===Original===
“You’ll take your toast and cocoa while they’re hot, dear, won’t you?” she said coaxingly.

===Chinese===
“吐司和可可趁热吃了吧，亲爱的，好么？”她柔声哄劝道。

===Original===
“Yes, I will, Phyllis, thank you,” Miss Bellingham answered. “I am only just going to take off my hat,” and she left the room, followed by the astonishingly transfigured spinster.

===Chinese===
“好，我吃，菲莉斯（Phyllis），谢谢你，”贝林厄姆小姐答道，“我这就去把帽子摘掉。”说着她离开了房间，那位判若两人、令人吃惊的老小姐跟了出去。

===Original===
She returned almost immediately as Mr. Bellingham was in the midst of a profound yawn, and sat down to her frugal meal, when her father mystified me considerably by remarking:

===Chinese===
她几乎立刻就回来了，那时贝林厄姆先生正打着一个大呵欠打到一半；她坐下开始那顿清简的晚餐，这时她父亲开口说了一句让我大惑不解的话：

===Original===
“You’re late tonight, chick. Have the Shepherd Kings been giving trouble?”

===Chinese===
“丫头，你今晚回来得晚啦。是牧人王（Shepherd Kings）们不听话了么？”

===Original===
“No,” she replied; “but I thought I might as well get them done. So I dropped in at the Ormond Street library on my way home and finished them.”

===Chinese===
“没有，”她答道，“不过我想不如索性把它们做完算了。所以回家路上我拐进了奥蒙德街（Ormond Street）的图书馆，把它们赶完了。”

===Original===
“Then they are ready for stuffing now?”

===Chinese===
“那么，它们现在可以拿去填充了？”

===Original===
“Yes.” As she answered she caught my astonished eye (for a stuffed Shepherd King is undoubtedly a somewhat surprising phenomenon) and laughed softly.

===Chinese===
“可以了。”她答话时瞥见我一脸惊愕（因为一只填充成标本的牧人王，无疑是桩相当惊人的现象），便轻轻笑了。

===Original===
“We mustn’t talk in riddles like this,” she said, “before Doctor Berkeley, or he will turn us both into pillars of salt. My father is referring to my work,” she explained to me.

===Chinese===
“咱们可不能这样打哑谜，”她说，“当着伯克利医生的面——不然他要把咱们俩统统变成盐柱呢。父亲说的是我的工作。”她向我解释道。

===Original===
“Are you a taxidermist, then?” I asked.

===Chinese===
“这么说，您是位标本剥制师？”我问。

===Original===
She hastily set down the cup that she was raising to her lips and broke into a ripple of quiet laughter.

===Chinese===
她慌忙放下正要举到唇边的杯子，迸出一串轻柔的笑声。

===Original===
“I am afraid my father has misled you with his irreverent expressions. He will have to atone by explaining.”

===Chinese===
“恐怕是父亲那些不敬的说法把您引入了歧途。他得把事情解释清楚，将功折罪。”

===Original===
“You see, Doctor,” said Mr. Bellingham, “Ruth is a literary searcher﻿—”

===Chinese===
“您瞧，医生，”贝林厄姆先生说，“露丝是个文学查证员——”

===Original===
“Oh, don’t call me a searcher!” Miss Bellingham protested. “It suggests the female searcher at a police station. Say investigator.”

===Chinese===
“哦，别管我叫查证员！”贝林厄姆小姐抗议道，“这听着像警察局里搜身的女搜查员。就叫调查员吧。”

===Original===
“Very well, investigator or investigatrix, if you like. She hunts up references and bibliographies at the Museum for people who are writing books. She looks up everything that has been written on a given subject, and then, when she has crammed herself to a bursting-point with facts, she goes to her client and disgorges and crams him or her, and he or she finally disgorges into the Press.”

===Chinese===
“很好，随你高兴，调查员或者女调查员。她在博物馆里替那些写书的人查考引文和书目。她把既定题目下前人写过的一切统统翻检出来，待到把自己填塞得饱胀欲裂，便去见她的主顾，把满腹的货色倾吐出来，填塞进他或她的肚里；他或她最后再朝出版界倾吐出去。”

===Original===
“What a disgusting way to put it!” said his daughter. “However, that is what it amounts to. I am a literary jackal, a collector of provender for the literary lions. Is that quite clear?”

===Chinese===
“这说法多倒人胃口！”他女儿说，“不过，归根结底就是这么回事。我是一头文学豺，专为文坛的狮子们采集口粮。这下清楚了吧？”

===Original===
“Perfectly. But I don’t think that, even now, I quite understand about the stuffed Shepherd Kings.”

===Chinese===
“再清楚不过。只是，即便到了这会儿，我对那填充起来的牧人王还是不大明白。”

===Original===
“Oh, it was not the Shepherd Kings who were to be stuffed. It was the author! That was mere obscurity of speech on the part of my father. The position is this: A venerable Archdeacon wrote an article on the patriarch Joseph﻿—”

===Chinese===
“哦，要填充的不是牧人王，是那位作者！那不过是我父亲言语含糊。情形是这样的：一位德高望重的会吏长（Archdeacon）写了一篇论族长约瑟（Joseph）的文章——”

===Original===
“And didn’t know anything about him,” interrupted Mr. Bellingham, “and got tripped up by a specialist who did, and then got shirty﻿—”

===Chinese===
“却对他一无所知，”贝林厄姆先生插嘴道，“被一位懂行的专家逮着了破绽，于是恼羞成怒——”

===Original===
“Nothing of the kind,” said Miss Bellingham. “He knew as much as venerable archdeacons ought to know; but the expert knew more. So the archdeacon commissioned me to collect the literature on the state of Egypt at the end of the seventeenth dynasty, which I have done; and tomorrow I shall go and stuff him, as my father expresses it, and then﻿—”

===Chinese===
“根本没有的事，”贝林厄姆小姐说，“德高的会吏长该知道的，他都知道；只是那位专家知道得更多。于是会吏长委托我把有关第十七王朝末期埃及状况的文献收集齐全，这我已经办完了；明天我就要去填充他——照我父亲的说法——然后——”

===Original===
“And then,” Mr. Bellingham interrupted, “the archdeacon will rush forth and pelt that expert with Shepherd Kings and Sequenen-Ra and the whole tag-rag and bobtail of the seventeenth dynasty. Oh, there’ll be wigs on the green, I can tell you.”

===Chinese===
“然后，”贝林厄姆先生打断道，“那位会吏长就会冲出去，拿牧人王啊、塞格嫩拉（Sequenen-Ra）啊，外加第十七王朝全套的乌合之众，劈头盖脑砸向那位专家。哦，我跟您说，到时候准有一场好架打。”

===Original===
“Yes, I expect there will be quite a skirmish,” said Miss Bellingham. And thus dismissing the subject she made an energetic attack on the toast while her father refreshed himself with a colossal yawn.

===Chinese===
“是啊，我料想会有一场相当热闹的小交锋。”贝林厄姆小姐说。就这样撇开了话头，她便劲头十足地向吐司发起进攻，她父亲则打了个其大无比的呵欠提神。

===Original===
I watched her with furtive admiration and deep and growing interest. In spite of her pallor, her weary eyes, and her drawn and almost haggard face, she was an exceedingly handsome girl; and there was in her aspect a suggestion of purpose, of strength and character that marked her off from the rank and file of womanhood. I noted this as I stole an occasional glance at her or turned to answer some remark addressed to me; and I noted, too, that her speech, despite a general undertone of depression, was yet not without a certain caustic, ironical humor. She was certainly a rather enigmatical young person, but very decidedly interesting.

===Chinese===
我怀着暗暗的倾慕和深沉而日益增长的兴味注视着她。尽管她面色苍白、目光倦怠、脸容瘦削得近乎憔悴，她仍是一位极其俊美的姑娘；她的气度中含有一种志向、一种力量与品格的意味，把她从寻常女子的行列中区别出来。我时而偷觑她一眼，时而转过身去应答人家对我说的话，把这一切都记在心里；我还注意到，她的话语虽然通篇透着一层低沉抑郁的底色，却不乏一种尖刻而带讥诮的幽默。她确实是个颇为费解的年轻女子，但又分明十分耐人寻味。

===Original===
When she had finished her repast she put aside the tray and, opening the shabby handbag, asked:

===Chinese===
她吃完那顿饭食，便把托盘移开，打开那只寒旧的手提包，问道：

===Original===
“Do you take any interest in Egyptian history? We are as mad as hatters on the subject. It seems to be a family complaint.”

===Chinese===
“您对埃及历史感兴趣么？我们家在这上头疯得跟帽匠似的。看来是种家传的毛病。”

===Original===
“I don’t know much about it,” I answered. “Medical studies are rather engrossing and don’t leave much time for general reading.”

===Chinese===
“我知道得不多，”我答道，“医学的功课太缠人，剩不下多少时间读闲书。”

===Original===
“Naturally,” she said. “You can’t specialize in everything. But if you would care to see how the business of a literary jackal is conducted, I will show you my notes.”

===Chinese===
“那是自然，”她说，“人不能样样都专。不过，您若想见识见识文学豺这门行当是怎么个干法，我就把我的笔记给您看。”

===Original===
I accepted the offer eagerly (not, I fear, from pure enthusiasm for the subject), and she brought forth from the bag four blue-covered, quarto notebooks, each dealing with one of the four dynasties from the fourteenth to the seventeenth. As I glanced through the neat and orderly extracts with which they were filled we discussed the intricacies of the peculiarly difficult and confused period that they covered, gradually lowering our voices as Mr. Bellingham’s eyes closed and his head fell against the back of his chair. We had just reached the critical reign of Apepa II when a resounding snore broke in upon the studious quiet of the room and sent us both into a fit of silent laughter.

===Chinese===
我热切地领了这番情（恐怕并非出于对科目本身的纯粹热忱）；她从提包里取出四本蓝皮的四开笔记本，每本各论从第十四到第十七这四个王朝中的一个。我翻检着本子里那些填得整整齐齐的摘录，我们便讨论起这几册所涵盖的那个格外艰深混乱时期的种种纠葛；贝林厄姆先生的眼睛渐渐阖上，脑袋垂到椅背上，我们的说话声也随之越放越低。刚谈到阿佩帕二世（Apepa II）那个关系重大的王朝，一声响亮的鼾声猛然搅破满屋书斋似的宁静，惹得我们俩憋着声笑作一团。

===Original===
“Your conversation has done its work,” she whispered as I stealthily picked up my hat, and together we stole on tiptoe to the door, which she opened without a sound. Once outside, she suddenly dropped her bantering manner and said quite earnestly:

===Chinese===
“您的谈话已经奏效了。”我悄悄拿起帽子时，她低声说；我们一起踮着脚尖溜到门口，她开门竟没有半点声响。一出到门外，她忽然收起那副打趣的神气，十分恳切地说：

===Original===
“How kind it was of you to come and see him tonight. You have done him a world of good, and I am most grateful. Good night!”

===Chinese===
“您今晚肯来看他，真是太难得了。您给他带来了说不尽的好处，我感激得很。晚安！”

===Original===
She shook hands with me really cordially, and I took my way down the creaking stairs in a whirl of happiness that I was quite at a loss to account for.

===Chinese===
她同我握手，握得由衷地亲切；我踏着嘎吱作响的楼梯走下去，心里卷着一片欢畅的旋涡，全然说不出个所以然来。

## V The Watercress-Bed / 五 水田芥田

===Original===
Barnard’s practise, like most others, was subject to those fluctuations that fill the struggling practitioner alternately with hope and despair. The work came in paroxysms with intervals of almost complete stagnation. One of these intermissions occurred on the day after my visit to Nevill’s Court, with the result that by half-past eleven I found myself wondering what I should do with the remainder of the day. The better to consider this weighty problem, I strolled down to the Embankment, and, leaning on the parapet, contemplated the view across the river; the gray stone bridge with its perspective of arches, the picturesque pile of the shot-towers, and beyond, the shadowy shapes of the Abbey and St. Stephen’s.

===Chinese===
巴纳德的那份诊业同大多数同行一样，总要受那种起伏波动的摆布，叫惨淡经营的开业医一会儿满怀希望，一会儿灰心绝望。诊务一阵一阵地涌来，间歇里却近乎完全停顿。我造访内维尔巷的第二天，恰逢这样一段间歇，结果到了十一点半，我已经在琢磨这一天剩下的光阴该怎么打发了。为了更好地考虑这个重大的问题，我踱步到泰晤士河堤，倚着栏杆，凝望河对岸的景致：灰色石桥连同那一孔孔桥拱的透视，铅弹塔那如画的一簇，再远处，是修道院（the Abbey）和圣斯蒂芬（St. Stephen's）朦胧的轮廓。

===Original===
It was a pleasant scene, restful and quiet, with a touch of life and a hint of sober romance, when a barge swept down through the middle arch of the bridge with a lugsail hoisted to a jury mast and a white-aproned woman at the tiller. Dreamily I watched the craft creep by upon the moving tide, noted the low freeboard, almost awash, the careful helmswoman, and the dog on the forecastle yapping at the distant shore﻿—and thought of Ruth Bellingham.

===Chinese===
这是一片宜人的景致，安宁静谧，带着一点生趣，透着一丝素淡的浪漫——一条驳船正从大桥中间的桥孔顺流而下，应急桅杆上挂着一面斜桁帆，舵柄旁立着个系白围裙的妇人。我恍恍惚惚地望着那船在流动的潮水上缓缓爬行，注意到它干舷低矮、几乎没入水中，掌舵的妇人小心翼翼，还有船头那条冲着远岸狂吠的狗——于是想起了露丝·贝林厄姆。

===Original===
What was there about this strange girl that had made so deep an impression on me? That was the question that I propounded to myself, and not for the first time. Of the fact itself there was no doubt. But what was the explanation? Was it her unusual surroundings? Her occupation and rather recondite learning? Her striking personality and exceptional good looks? Or her connection with the dramatic mystery of her lost uncle?

===Chinese===
这个古怪的姑娘身上到底有什么，竟在我心里留下这么深的印痕？这是我向自己提出的问题，而且不是头一回了。这印象本身的存在毫无疑义。可是缘故何在呢？是她那不同寻常的境况？她的职业和相当冷僻的学问？她那出众的品性与过人的美貌？还是她同她那位失踪伯父的戏剧性疑案之间的干系？

===Original===
I concluded that it was all of these. Everything connected with her was unusual and arresting; but over and above these circumstances there was a certain sympathy and personal affinity of which I was strongly conscious and of which I dimly hoped that she, perhaps, was a little conscious too. At any rate, I was deeply interested in her; of that there was no doubt whatever. Short as our acquaintance had been, she held a place in my thoughts that had never been held by any other woman.

===Chinese===
我的结论是：样样都是。凡与她相关的一切，无不异乎寻常、引人注目；可是在这些情形之外，还有一种心意的投契和性情的相引，这一点我自己感觉得再清楚不过，还隐约希望她——或者——也稍稍感到了一点。无论如何，我对她是深切关注的；这一点毫无疑义。我们的相识尽管短浅，她在我心念里占据的位置，却是从来没有任何女子占据过的。

===Original===
From Ruth Bellingham my reflections passed by a natural transition to the curious story that her father had told me. It was a queer affair, that ill-drawn will, with the baffled lawyer protesting in the background. It almost seemed as if there must be something behind it all, especially when I remembered Mr. Hurst’s very singular proposal. But it was out of my depth; it was a case for a lawyer, and to a lawyer it should go. This very night, I resolved, I would go to Thorndyke and give him the whole story as it had been told to me.

===Chinese===
从露丝·贝林厄姆，我的思绪经过一番自然的过渡，转到了她父亲讲给我听的那个离奇的故事。那是一桩古怪的事——那份拟得拙劣的遗嘱，外加那位无计可施的律师在幕后连声抗议。这一切背后几乎显得必定另藏着什么，尤其是我想起赫斯特先生那个非常古怪的提议之后。可这超出了我的学识；这是律师的案子，也该归律师去办。我拿定主意：就在今晚，我要去桑代克那里，把这故事照人家讲给我的原样，一五一十讲给他听。

===Original===
And then there happened one of those coincidences at which we all wonder when they occur, but which are so frequent as to have become enshrined in a proverb. For even as I formed the resolution, I observed two men approaching from the direction of Blackfriars, and recognized in them my quondam teacher and his junior.

===Chinese===
接着便发生了那种巧合——事情发生时我们人人称奇，可它又频繁得早已被供奉进谚语里去了。因为正当我下定决心的当儿，我望见两个人从黑衣修士（Blackfriars）的方向走来，认出他们正是我昔日的老师和他的那位后辈。

===Original===
“I was just thinking about you,” I said as they came up.

===Chinese===
“我正想着你们呢。”他们走上前来时我说。

===Original===
“Very flattering,” replied Jervis; “but I thought you had to talk of the devil.”

===Chinese===
“真是承蒙抬爱，”杰维斯答道，“不过我记得那句老话，说的是你总得‘谈起’魔鬼才行呀。”

===Original===
“Perhaps,” suggested Thorndyke, “he was talking to himself. But why were you thinking of us, and what was the nature of your thoughts?”

===Chinese===
“也许，”桑代克提点道，“他这是在自言自语呢。不过，你为什么想着我们？想的又是些什么性质的心事？”

===Original===
“My thoughts had reference to the Bellingham case. I spent the whole of last evening at Nevill’s Court.”

===Chinese===
“我想的是贝林厄姆家的案子。昨晚我整个傍晚都待在内维尔巷。”

===Original===
“Ha! And are there any fresh developments?”

===Chinese===
“哈！有什么新进展么？”

===Original===
“Yes, by Jove! there are. Bellingham gave me a full detailed description of the will; and a pretty document it seems to be.”

===Chinese===
“有，天哪！真有。贝林厄姆把那份遗嘱详详细细、原原本本地给我讲了一遍；那看来真是一份‘了不起’的文书。”

===Original===
“Did he give you permission to repeat the details to me?”

===Chinese===
“他准许你把细节向我复述么？”

===Original===
“Yes. I asked specifically if I might, and he had no objection whatever.”

===Chinese===
“准许了。我特意问了可不可以讲，他毫无异议。”

===Original===
“Good. We are lunching at Soho today as Polton has his hands full. Come with us and share our table and tell us your story as we go. Will that suit you?”

===Chinese===
“好。今天波尔顿忙得不可开交，我们就在苏活区（Soho）吃午饭。跟我们一起去，同我们拼一张桌子，一路把你的故事讲给我们听。这样合你的意么？”

===Original===
It suited me admirably in the present state of the practise, and I accepted the invitation with undissembled glee.

===Chinese===
在眼下诊务的这种状况下，这安排再合我意不过，我便毫不掩饰满心的欢喜，接受了邀请。

===Original===
“Very well,” said Thorndyke; “then let us walk slowly and finish with matters confidential before we plunge into the maddening crowd.”

===Chinese===
“很好，”桑代克说，“那我们就慢慢走，赶在一头扎进那昏乱鼎沸的人潮之前，把机密事项了结。”

===Original===
We set forth at a leisurely pace along the broad pavement and I commenced my narration. As well as I could remember, I related the circumstances that had led up to the present disposition of the property and then proceeded to the actual provisions of the will; to all of which my two friends listened with rapt interest, Thorndyke occasionally stopping me to jot down a memorandum in his pocketbook.

===Chinese===
我们沿着宽阔的人行道从容出发，我开始叙述。我凭记忆所及，把造成眼下这份产业处置的种种情由讲了一遍，接着讲到遗嘱的实际条款；两位朋友听得入神，桑代克不时叫住我，好在袖珍本里记下一笔备忘。

===Original===
“Why, the fellow must have been a stark lunatic!” Jervis exclaimed, when I had finished. “He seems to have laid himself out with the most devilish ingenuity to defeat his own ends.”

===Chinese===
“嗬，这家伙准是个十足的疯子！”我讲完之后，杰维斯叫道，“他似乎是存心拿出最恶魔式的巧思，来挫败自己的目的。”

===Original===
“That is not an uncommon peculiarity with testators,” Thorndyke remarked. “A direct and perfectly intelligible will is rather the exception. But we can hardly judge until we have seen the actual document. I suppose Bellingham hasn’t a copy?”

===Chinese===
“立遗嘱的人有这种脾气的可不算稀奇，”桑代克说，“一份直截了当、明白晓畅的遗嘱反倒算是例外。不过，没有见到正式文书之前，我们很难下判断。我想贝林厄姆手里没有副本吧？”

===Original===
“I don’t know,” said I; “but I will ask him.”

===Chinese===
“我不知道，”我说，“不过我会问他的。”

===Original===
“If he has one, I should like to look through it,” said Thorndyke. “The provisions are very peculiar, and, as Jervis says, admirably calculated to defeat the testator’s wishes if they have been correctly reported. And, apart from that, they have a remarkable bearing on the circumstances of the disappearance. I daresay you noticed that.”

===Chinese===
“他若有一份，我倒想从头至尾读一遍，”桑代克说，“这些条款非常特别，而且，正如杰维斯所说，倘若转述无误，它们简直是以可观的巧思注定要挫败立遗嘱人的心愿。撇开这一点，它们同失踪案的种种情形还有一层显著的关系。我敢说你注意到了。”

===Original===
“I noticed that it is very much to Hurst’s advantage that the body has not been found.”

===Chinese===
“我注意到，遗体一直没有找到，这对赫斯特非常有利。”

===Original===
“Yes, of course. But there are some other points that are very significant. However, it would be premature to discuss the terms of the will until we have seen the actual document or a certified copy.”

===Chinese===
“是的，当然。不过另外还有几点也十分要紧。然而，在见到正式文书或核证副本之前就讨论遗嘱条款，未免为时过早。”

===Original===
“If there is a copy extant,” I said, “I will try to get hold of it. But Bellingham is terribly afraid of being suspected of a desire to get professional advice gratis.”

===Chinese===
“倘若真还存有一份副本，”我说，“我会设法弄到手。只是贝林厄姆生怕人家疑心他想白讨行家的主意。”

===Original===
“That,” said Thorndyke, “is natural enough, and not discreditable. But you must overcome his scruples somehow. I expect you will be able to. You are a plausible young gentleman, as I remember of old, and you seem to have established yourself as quite the friend of the family.”

===Chinese===
“这个嘛，”桑代克说，“再自然不过，也说不上丢人。可他的顾虑，你总得想个法子打消。我料想你有这个能耐。你是个能说会道的年轻绅士——我记得你当年就是这样——而且看来你俨然已经坐稳了这一家朋友的位置。”

===Original===
“They are rather interesting people,” I explained; “very cultivated and with a strong leaning toward archeology. It seems to be in the blood.”

===Chinese===
“他们是相当有意思的人家，”我解释道，“极有教养，对考古学癖好极深。这癖好仿佛是渗在血里的。”

===Original===
“Yes,” said Thorndyke; “a family tendency, probably due to contact and common surroundings rather than heredity. So you like Godfrey Bellingham?”

===Chinese===
“是啊，”桑代克说，“一种家族倾向，多半出于耳濡目染和共同的环境，而非遗传。这么说，你喜欢戈弗雷·贝林厄姆？”

===Original===
“Yes. He is a trifle peppery and impulsive but quite an agreeable, genial old buffer.”

===Chinese===
“喜欢。他有点火爆脾气，容易冲动，可是个十分可亲、和和气气的老家伙。”

===Original===
“And the daughter,” said Jervis, “what is she like?”

===Chinese===
“那么那位女儿，”杰维斯说，“她是个什么样的人？”

===Original===
“Oh, she is a learned lady; works up bibliographies and references at the Museum.”

===Chinese===
“哦，她是位有学问的女士；在博物馆里编制书目、查考文献。”

===Original===
“Ah!” Jervis exclaimed, with disfavor, “I know the breed. Inky fingers; no chest to speak of; all side and spectacles.”

===Chinese===
“啊！”杰维斯大不以为然地叫道，“这一路人我认得。指头上墨迹斑斑；胸脯没什么可提的；浑身傲气，外加一副眼镜。”

===Original===
I rose artlessly at the gross and palpable bait.

===Chinese===
我老老实实地扑向了这粗劣而露骨的钓饵。

===Original===
“You’re quite wrong,” I exclaimed indignantly, contrasting Jervis’s hideous presentment with the comely original. “She is an exceedingly good-looking girl, and her manners all that a lady’s should be. A little stiff, perhaps, but then I am only an acquaintance﻿—almost a stranger.”

===Chinese===
“你全说错了，”我愤然叫道，一面把杰维斯那副狰狞的画像同秀美的原型两相对照，“她是位相貌极其标致的姑娘，举止处处是一位淑女所应有的。也许有点拘谨，可我终究只是个相识——差不多算个陌生人。”

===Original===
“But,” Jervis persisted, “what is she like, in appearance I mean. Short? fat? sandy? Give us intelligible details.”

===Chinese===
“可是，”杰维斯追问不休，“她长得什么样？我是说模样。矮？胖？沙色头发？给我们些明白易懂的细节。”

===Original===
I made a rapid mental inventory, assisted by my recent cogitations.

===Chinese===
借着方才那一番凝想之助，我在心里飞快地清点了一遍。

===Original===
“She is about five feet seven, slim but rather plump, very erect in carriage and graceful in movements; black hair, loosely parted in the middle and falling very prettily away from the forehead; pale, clear complexion, dark gray eyes, straight eyebrows, straight, well-shaped nose, short mouth, rather full; round chin﻿—what the deuce are you grinning at, Jervis?” For my friend had suddenly unmasked his batteries and now threatened, like the Cheshire cat, to dissolve into a mere abstraction of amusement.

===Chinese===
“她身高约五英尺七，苗条却颇丰润，体态十分挺拔，举止优雅；黑发，从中间松松分开，从额前披垂下来，非常好看；面色白净，深灰色的眼睛，端直的眉毛，端直而秀气的鼻子，嘴偏短，唇却相当丰满；圆下巴——你到底在龇着牙笑什么，杰维斯？”因为我这位朋友忽然亮出了他的全部炮阵，此刻大有柴郡猫（Cheshire cat）的势头，眼看要溶化成一团纯粹的笑意。

===Original===
“If there is a copy of that will, Thorndyke,” he said, “we shall get it. I think you agree with me, reverend senior?”

===Chinese===
“桑代克，那遗嘱的副本若真存在，我们准能到手。我想你同意我的话吧，可敬的前辈？”

===Original===
“I have already said,” was the reply, “that I put my trust in Berkeley. And now let us dismiss professional topics. This is our hostelry.”

===Chinese===
回答是：“我早就说过，我把信托放在伯克利身上。现在咱们把本行的话题撇下吧。咱们的饭馆到了。”

===Original===
He pushed open an unpretentious glazed door, and we followed him into the restaurant, whereof the atmosphere was pervaded by an appetizing meatiness mingled with less agreeable suggestions of the destructive distillation of fat.

===Chinese===
他推开一扇不事张扬的玻璃门，我们跟着他走进那家餐馆；馆内的空气里弥漫着开胃的肉香，又混杂着些不那么惬意的、油脂干馏般的气息。

===Original===
It was some two hours later when I wished my friends adieu under the golden-leaved plane trees of King’s Bench Walk.

===Chinese===
约莫两个钟头之后，我在王座步道金叶灿灿的梧桐树下向两位朋友道了别。

===Original===
“I won’t ask you to come in now,” said Thorndyke, “as we have some consultations this afternoon. But come in and see us soon; don’t wait for that copy of the will.”

===Chinese===
“这会儿我就不请你进来了，”桑代克说，“我们下午还有几桩会商。不过过不了几天就来看看我们；别等什么遗嘱副本。”

===Original===
“No,” said Jervis. “Drop in in the evening when your work is done; unless, of course, there is more attractive society elsewhere. Oh, you needn’t turn that color, my dear child; we have all been young once; there is even a tradition that Thorndyke was young some time back in the pre-dynastic period.”

===Chinese===
“可不是，”杰维斯说，“晚上干完了活就随便过来坐坐；当然喽，除非别处有更招人喜欢的交际。哦，用不着变那个脸色，亲爱的孩子；咱们谁没年轻过呢；甚至还有个传说，说桑代克早在前王朝时期还年轻过一回。”

===Original===
“Don’t take any notice of him, Berkeley,” said Thorndyke. “The eggshell is sticking to his head still. He’ll know better when he is my age.”

===Chinese===
“别理他，伯克利，”桑代克说，“他脑壳上还粘着蛋壳呢。等他到了我这把年纪，就明白多了。”

===Original===
“Methuselah!” exclaimed Jervis. “I hope I shan’t have to wait as long as that!”

===Chinese===
“玛土撒拉（Methuselah）！”杰维斯叫道，“但愿我用不着等那么久！”

===Original===
Thorndyke smiled benevolently at his irrepressible junior, and, shaking my hand cordially, turned into the entry.

===Chinese===
桑代克对这位按捺不住的后辈宽厚地一笑，热诚地同我握过手，转身进了门厅。

===Original===
From the Temple I wended northward, to the adjacent College of Surgeons, where I spent a couple of profitable hours examining the “pickles” and refreshing my memory on the subjects of pathology and anatomy; marveling afresh (as every practical anatomist must marvel) at the incredibly perfect technique of the dissections, and inwardly paying tribute to the founder of the collection. At length the warning of the clock, combined with an increasing craving for tea, drove me forth and bore me toward the scene of my not very strenuous labors. My mind was still occupied with the contents of the cases and the great glass jars, so that I found myself at the corner of Fetter Lane without a very clear idea of how I had got there. But at that point I was aroused from my reflections rather abruptly by a raucous voice in my ear.

===Chinese===
从内殿出发，我折向北方，前往毗邻的外科医师学会（College of Surgeons），在那里度过了足有两个钟头获益不浅的时光，细细察看那些“泡菜”，把病理学和解剖学两门课业在记忆里温习了一遍；对那些解剖手法不可思议的完美，重新叹服了一番（凡是亲手做过解剖的人都不能不叹服），并在心里向这批收藏的奠基人致敬。末了，闭馆钟声的告诫，加上一阵愈来愈强的对茶的渴望，把我驱出门外，送往我那份并不怎么辛苦的劳作之所。我的心仍被那些陈列柜和大玻璃坛里的东西占着，以致走到菲特巷口，对自己是怎么走来的都没有个很清楚的概念。可就在那儿，一声嘶哑的嗓音在我耳边炸响，猛地把我从沉思里拽了出来。

===Original===
“ ’Orrible discovery at Sidcup!”

===Chinese===
“锡德卡普惊现恐怖发现哪！”

===Original===
I turned wrathfully﻿—for a London street-boy’s yell, let off at point-blank range, is, in effect, like the smack of an open hand﻿—but the inscription on the staring yellow poster that was held up for my inspection changed my anger to curiosity.

===Chinese===
我怒气冲冲地转过身去——因为伦敦街头顽童的一声叫喊在近距离放出来，实际上就像挨了一记响亮的耳光——可是那举到我跟前、扎眼的黄色招贴上的字样，把我的怒火换成了好奇。

===Original===
“Horrible discovery in a watercress-bed!”

===Chinese===
“水田芥田里惊现恐怖发现！”

===Original===
Now, let prigs deny it if they will, but there is something very attractive in a “horrible discovery.” It hints at tragedy, at mystery, at romance. It promises to bring into our gray and commonplace life that element of the dramatic which is the salt that our existence is savored withal. “In a watercress-bed,” too! The rusticity of the background seemed to emphasize the horror of the discovery, whatever it might be.

===Chinese===
话说回来，道学先生们尽可以否认去，可“恐怖发现”这东西自有一股莫大的吸引力。它暗示着悲剧、疑案与浪漫。它许诺要把那戏剧性的要素带进我们灰色而平庸的生活——那正是给我们的人生调味的盐。何况还是“在水田芥田里”！乡野的背景似乎更把这项发现——不管它究竟是什么——的恐怖衬托得格外突出。

===Original===
I bought a copy of the paper, and, tucking it under my arm, hurried on to the surgery, promising myself a mental feast of watercress; but as I opened the door I found myself confronted by a corpulent woman of piebald and pimply aspect who saluted me with a deep groan. It was the lady from the coal shop in Fleur-de-Lys Court.

===Chinese===
我买了一份报纸，往腋下一夹，匆匆赶回诊所，心里许给自己一顿水田芥的精神盛筵；谁知一开门，就撞见一位胖大的妇人，面皮花斑、粉刺累累，冲我发出一声深沉的呻吟，算是见礼。来者正是百合花院煤店的那位太太。

===Original===
“Good evening, Mrs. Jablett,” I said briskly; “not come about yourself, I hope.”

===Chinese===
“晚上好，贾布利特太太，”我爽利地说，“但愿不是您自个儿不舒服。”

===Original===
“Yes, I have,” she answered, rising and following me gloomily into the consulting-room; and then, when I had seated her in the patient’s chair and myself at the writing table, she continued: “It’s my inside, you know, doctor.”

===Chinese===
“是啊，正是俺自个儿，”她答道，站起身，闷闷不乐地跟我进了诊室；待我在病人椅上安顿了她、自己在写字台前坐定，她接着说道：“大夫，是俺这肚子里的事儿，您知道的。”

===Original===
The statement lacked anatomical precision and merely excluded the domain of the skin specialist. I accordingly waited for enlightenment and speculated on the watercress-beds, while Mrs. Jablett regarded me expectantly with a dim and watery eye.

===Chinese===
这句话在解剖学上欠些精确，只把皮肤专家的领地排除在外。于是我静候开导，一面在心里推究水田芥田的种种；贾布利特太太则用一只水汪汪的浑眼睛期待地望着我。

===Original===
“Ah!” I said at length; “it’s your﻿—your inside, is it, Mrs. Jablett?”

===Chinese===
“啊！”我终于开口道，“是您那——那肚子里，是么，贾布利特太太？”

===Original===
“Yus. *And* my ’ead,” she added, with a voluminous sigh that filled the apartment with odorous reminiscences of “unsweetened.”

===Chinese===
“是哩。*还有*俺的脑袋，”她补了一句，随着一声饱满的长叹，满屋子都充满了“不加糖”烧酒的气味追忆。

===Original===
“Your head aches, does it?”

===Chinese===
“您头疼，是么？”

===Original===
“Somethink chronic!” said Mrs. Jablett. “Feels as if it was a-opening and a-shutting, a-opening and a-shutting, and when I sit down I feel as if I should *bust*.”

===Chinese===
“疼得邪乎着呢！”贾布利特太太说，“就觉得它一开一合、一开一合的，俺一坐下，就觉着自个儿要*炸*啦。”

===Original===
This picturesque description of her sensations﻿—not wholly inconsistent with her figure﻿—gave the clue to Mrs. Jablett’s sufferings. Resisting a frivolous impulse to reassure her as to the elasticity of the human integument, I considered her case in exhaustive detail, coasting delicately round the subject of “unsweetened” and finally sent her away, revived in spirits and grasping a bottle of *Mist. Sodae cum Bismutho* from Barnard’s big stock-jar. Then I went back to investigate the Horrible Discovery; but before I could open the paper, another patient arrived (*Impetigo contagiosa*, this time, affecting the “wide and arched-front sublime” of a juvenile Fetter Laner), and then yet another, and so on through the evening until at last I forgot the watercress-beds altogether. It was only when I had purified myself from the evening consultations with hot water and a nailbrush and was about to sit down to a frugal supper, that I remembered the newspaper and fetched it from the drawer of the consulting-room table, where it had been hastily thrust out of sight. I folded it into a convenient form, and, standing it upright against the water-jug, read the report at my ease as I supped.

===Chinese===
她对自身感觉的这番绘声绘色的描述——同她的身段倒也并不全然脱节——点出了贾布利特太太病痛的线索。我按下一个轻佻的冲动，没有就人体皮肤的弹性向她宽心安抚，只把她的病例不厌其详地斟酌了一番，在“不加糖”这个话题外围小心翼翼地绕行，末了打发她走时，她精神已然一振，手里攥着一瓶从巴纳德的大储药坛里取出的苏打铋合剂（*Mist. Sodae cum Bismutho*）。随后我回头去查究那桩恐怖发现；可是报纸还没能打开，又来了一位病人（这回是*触染性脓疱疮*，长在菲特巷一个少年那“宽广而拱起的堂皇前额”上），接着又来一位，如此这般地过了一晚上，到末了我把水田芥田忘了个干净。直到我用热水和指甲刷把一身晚间诊病的秽气洗净、正要坐下用一顿清简的晚饭时，才记起那份报纸，把它从诊室桌子的抽屉里取了出来——方才它是被匆匆塞进那里避人耳目的。我把它折成顺手的形状，竖着支在水壶旁边，一边吃一边从容地读那篇报道。

===Original===
There was plenty of it. Evidently the reporter had regarded it as a “scoop,” and the editor had backed him up with ample space and hair-raising headlines.

===Chinese===
报道的篇幅真不少。显然，记者把它当作一桩“独家新闻”，编辑也拿充足的版面和耸人听闻的大标题给他撑腰。

===Original===
> A startling discovery was made yesterday afternoon in the course of clearing out a watercress-bed near the erstwhile rural village of Sidcup in Kent; a discovery that will occasion many a disagreeable qualm to those persons who have been in the habit of regaling themselves with this refreshing esculent. But before proceeding to a description of the circumstances of the actual discovery or of the objects found﻿—which, however, it may be stated at once, are nothing more or less than the fragments of a dismembered human body﻿—it will be interesting to trace the remarkable chain of coincidences by virtue of which the discovery was made.

===Chinese===
> 昨天下午，在肯特郡昔日犹是乡村的锡德卡普近旁，清理一片水田芥田时，有了一项惊人的发现——它将使那些惯以这种爽口菜蔬清口解馋的人士，平添许多不快的疑虑。但在进而描述实际发现的情形或所发现的物件之前——不过关于这些物件，不妨立刻言明，它们不是别的，正是一具被肢解的人体的碎块——先来追索一番那条使此项发现得以成就的了不起的巧合之链，倒也饶有兴味。

===Original===
> The beds in question have been laid out in a small artificial lake fed by a tiny streamlet which forms one of the numerous tributaries of the River Cray. Its depth is greater than usual in the watercress-beds, otherwise the gruesome relics could never have been concealed beneath its surface, and the flow of water through it, though continuous is slow. The tributary streamlet meanders through a succession of pasture meadows, in one of which the beds themselves are situated, and here throughout most of the year the fleecy victims of the human carnivore carry on the industry of converting grass into mutton. Now it happened some years ago that the sheep frequenting these pastures became affected with the disease known as “liver-rot”; and here we must make a short digression into the domain of pathology.

===Chinese===
> 出事的这片田床开辟在一座小型人工湖里，湖水由一条小小的溪流补给，那溪流是克雷河（River Cray）为数众多的支流之一。湖深超过水田芥田的寻常深度，否则那些阴森的遗物断无可能在水面之下藏匿至今；湖水流动虽不间断，却甚迟缓。这条支流蜿蜒穿过一连串牧草场，田床本身就坐落在其中一片草场上；一年里的大部分时光，那些毛茸茸的、供养人类这食肉兽的受害者们，便在此地从事着把青草转化为羊肉的营生。话说数年之前，常来这些草场的羊群染上了名为“肝蛭病”的疾患——说到这里，我们得往病理学的领域里作一次短短的岔游。

===Original===
> Liver-rot is a disease of quite romantic antecedents. Its cause is a small, flat worm﻿—the liver-fluke﻿—which infests the liver and bile-ducts of the affected sheep.

===Chinese===
> 肝蛭病是一种来历颇具浪漫色彩的疾病。它的病因是一条扁平的小虫——肝蛭——寄生在病羊的肝脏和胆管里。

===Original===
> Now how does the worm get into the sheep’s liver? That is where the romance comes in. Let us see.

===Chinese===
> 那么，这条虫是怎么进到羊的肝脏里去的呢？浪漫便从这里登场。且看。

===Original===
> The cycle of transformation begins with the deposit of the eggs of the fluke in some shallow stream or ditch running through pasture lands. Now each egg has a sort of lid, which presently opens and lets out a minute, hairy creature who swims away in search of a particular kind of water-snail﻿—the kind called by naturalists *Limnoea truncatula*. If he finds a snail, he bores his way into its flesh and soon begins to grow and wax fat. Then he brings forth a family﻿—of tiny worms quite unlike himself, little creatures called rediae, which soon give birth to families of young rediae. So they go on for several generations, but at last there comes a generation of rediae which, instead of giving birth to fresh rediae, produce families of totally different offspring; big-headed, long-tailed creatures like miniature tadpoles, called by the learned cercariae. The cercariae soon wriggle their way out of the body of the snail, and then complications arise: for it is the habit of this particular snail to leave the water occasionally and take a stroll in the fields. Thus the cercariae, escaping from the snail find themselves on the grass whereupon they promptly drop their tails and stick themselves to the grass-blades. Then comes the unsuspecting sheep to take his frugal meal, and, cropping the grass swallows it, cercariae and all. But the latter, when they find themselves in the sheep’s stomach, make their way straight to the bile-ducts, up which they travel to the liver. Here, in a few weeks, they grow up into full-blown flukes and begin the important business of producing eggs.

===Chinese===
> 这一番变态的轮回，始于肝蛭把卵产在某条流经牧场的浅溪或沟渠之中。每粒卵上有一个小小的盖，不久便会开启，放出一个小小的、周身纤毛的家伙，游开去寻找一种特定的水螺——就是博物学家称作 *Limnoea truncatula*（截形泥蜗螺）的那一种。它若寻得一只螺，便钻进螺肉里去，不久便开始滋长、养肥。随后它养育出一窝儿女——一群同它自己全然不似的小虫，小家伙们唤作雷蚴（rediae）；雷蚴不久又生出一窝窝小雷蚴。如此绵延数代，末了终于有一代雷蚴，不再生育新的雷蚴，而是产下全然不同的后裔：大脑袋、长尾巴、形似微型蝌蚪的小东西，学者们称之为尾蚴（cercariae）。尾蚴不久便从螺体里扭摆而出，这时节外生枝了：因为这种水螺素有偶尔离水登岸、到田野间遛弯的习性。于是，逃出螺体的尾蚴发觉自己落在了草上，便当机立断甩掉尾巴，把自己粘在草叶上。接着，毫无戒心的羊来用它那顿清简的饭食，一口把草啃下，连草带尾蚴吞进肚去。而尾蚴一发觉自己身处羊胃，便径直奔赴胆管，溯管而上，进入肝脏。在这里，不出几个星期，它们便长成发育完全的肝蛭，着手进行产卵这桩重大的事业。

===Original===
> Such is the pathological romance of the liver-rot; and now what is its connection with this mysterious discovery? It is this. After the outbreak of liver-rot above referred to, the ground landlord, a Mr. John Bellingham, instructed his solicitor to insert a clause in the lease of the beds directing that the latter should be periodically cleared and examined by an expert to make sure that they were free from the noxious water-snails. The last lease expired about two years ago, and since then the beds have been out of cultivation; but, for the safety of the adjacent pastures, it was considered necessary to make the customary periodical inspection, and it was in the course of cleaning the beds for this purpose that the present discovery was made.

===Chinese===
> 肝蛭病的病理罗曼史便是如此；那么它同这项神秘的发现又有何干系呢？干系在此。上文所说的肝蛭病爆发之后，地产业主约翰·贝林厄姆先生吩咐他的律师，在田床的租约中加入一条条款，责成承租人定期清理田床并延请专家查验，以确保那些有害的水螺绝迹。上一份租约约于两年前满期，此后田床便一直荒废未种；但为了邻近牧场的安全，例行的定期查验仍被视为必不可少，而正是在为此目的清理田床之际，有了眼下这项发现。

===Original===
> The operation began two days ago. A gang of three men proceeded systematically to grub up the plants and collect the multitudes of water-snails that they might be examined by the expert to see if any obnoxious species were present. They had cleared nearly half of the beds when, yesterday afternoon, one of the men working in the deepest part came upon some bones, the appearance of which excited his suspicion. Thereupon he called his mates, and they carefully picked away the plants piecemeal, a process that soon laid bare an unmistakable human hand lying on the mud amongst the roots. Fortunately they had the wisdom not to disturb the remains, but at once sent off a message to the police. Very soon, an inspector and a sergeant, accompanied by the divisional surgeon, arrived on the scene, and were able to view the remains lying as they had been found. And now another very strange fact came to light; for it was seen that the hand﻿—a left one﻿—lying on the mud was minus its third finger. This is regarded by the police as a very important fact as bearing on the question of identification, seeing that the number of persons having the third finger of the left hand missing must be quite small. After a thorough examination on the spot, the bones were carefully collected and conveyed to the mortuary, where they now lie awaiting further inquiries.

===Chinese===
> 清理工程始于两天之前。三名工人组成一班，按部就班地把植株连根刨起，把成群的水螺收集拢来，好交专家查验其中有无有害的种类。到昨天下午，田床已清出将近一半，在最深一段作业的一名工人碰上了几根骨头，骨头的样子引起了他的疑心。于是他唤来同伴，他们小心翼翼地把植株一点一点摘除，这道工序很快便使一只确凿无疑的人手显露出来，横陈在根须之间的淤泥上。所幸他们有那份见识，没有扰动遗骨，而是立刻给警方送了信。很快，一位探长和一名警士在警区医生（divisional surgeon）的陪同下赶到现场，得以察看保持发现时原状的遗骨。这时又有一桩极奇怪的事实水落石出：人们看到，那只手——一只左手——横卧泥上，独缺第三根手指。警方认为，此事于辨认身份的问题干系重大，因为左手缺少第三指的人数必定寥寥无几。经过就地的一番彻底查验，骨殖被小心收集起来，运往停尸房，现在正停放在那里，听候进一步的调查。

===Original===
> The divisional surgeon, Dr. Brandon, in an interview with our representative, made the following statements:

===Chinese===
> 警区医生布兰登（Brandon）接受本报代表采访时，作了如下陈述：

===Original===
> “The bones are those of the left arm of a middle-aged or elderly man about five feet eight inches in height. All the bones of the arm are present, including the scapula, or shoulder-blade, and the clavicle, or collarbone, but the three bones of the third finger are missing.”

===Chinese===
> “这些骨头属于一名中年或上了年纪的男子，身高约五英尺八英寸。臂部的骨头一应俱全，包括肩胛骨和锁骨，唯独第三指的三块骨头缺失。”

===Original===
> “Is this a deformity or has the finger been cut off?” our correspondent asked.

===Chinese===
> “这是先天畸形，还是那根手指被切除了？”本报记者问。

===Original===
> “The finger has been amputated,” was the reply. “If it had been absent from birth, the corresponding hand bone, or metacarpal, would have been wanting or deformed, whereas it is present and quite normal.”

===Chinese===
> “手指是被截断的，”回答是，“倘若生来缺失，手上相应的骨头——掌骨——便会缺如或畸形，而它现在不但存在，而且完全正常。”

===Original===
> “How long have the bones been in the water?” was the next question.

===Chinese===
> “这些骨头在水里有多久了？”是下一个问题。

===Original===
> “More than a year, I should say. They are quite clean; there is not a vestige of the soft structures left.”

===Chinese===
> “我看不止一年了。它们相当干净；软组织连一丝痕迹也不曾留下。”

===Original===
> “Have you any theory as to how the arm came to be deposited where it was found?”

===Chinese===
> “关于这条手臂何以会被放置在发现它的地方，您有什么推想么？”

===Original===
> “I should rather not answer that question,” was the guarded response.

===Chinese===
> “那个问题我还是不回答为好，”是那句谨守分寸的答复。

===Original===
> “One more question,” our correspondent urged. “The ground landlord, Mr. John Bellingham; is he not the gentleman who disappeared so mysteriously some time ago?”

===Chinese===
> “再问一个问题，”本报记者敦促道，“地产业主约翰·贝林厄姆先生——他不就是前些时候神秘失踪的那位先生么？”

===Original===
> “So I understand,” Dr. Brandon replied.

===Chinese===
> “据我所知正是，”布兰登医生答道。

===Original===
> “Can you tell me if Mr. Bellingham had lost the third finger of his left hand?”

===Chinese===
> “您能不能告诉我，贝林厄姆先生是否失去了左手的第三指？”

===Original===
> “I cannot say,” said Dr. Brandon; and he added with a smile, “you had better ask the police.”

===Chinese===
> “这我说不上，”布兰登医生说；他又含笑补了一句，“你们最好去问警方。”

===Original===
> That is how the matter stands at present. But we understand that the police are making active inquiries for any missing man who has lost the third finger of his left hand, and if any of our readers know of such a person, they are earnestly requested to communicate at once, either with us or the authorities.

===Chinese===
> 眼下事情便是如此。但我们了解到，警方正在积极查访任何左手失去第三指的失踪男子；读者诸君中若有知晓这般之人的，务请即刻同本报或当局联系。

===Original===
> Also we believe that a systematic search is to be made for further remains.

===Chinese===
> 此外，我们获悉还将进行一次系统搜索，以寻找其余的遗骸。

===Original===
I laid the newspaper down and fell into a train of reflection. It was certainly a most mysterious affair. The thought that had evidently come to the reporter’s mind stole naturally into mine. Could these remains be those of John Bellingham? It was obviously possible, though I could not but see that the fact of the bones having been found on his land, while it undoubtedly furnished the suggestion, did not in any way add to its probability. The connection was accidental and in nowise relevant.

===Chinese===
我放下报纸，陷入了一串沉思。这确乎是一桩极其神秘的事。那个显然已钻进记者脑中的念头，也自然而然地溜进了我的脑子。这些遗骸会不会是约翰·贝林厄姆的？这显然是可能的，尽管我不由得看到：骨殖是在他的地产上发现的这一事实，固然无疑提供了这层联想，却丝毫没有增添它的可能性。这层联系纯属偶然，毫不相干。

===Original===
Then, too, there was the missing finger. No reference to any such deformity had been made in the original report of the disappearance, though it could hardly have been overlooked. But it was useless to speculate without facts. I should be seeing Thorndyke in the course of the next few days, and, undoubtedly, if the discovery had any bearing upon the disappearance of John Bellingham, I should hear of it. With such a reflection I rose from the table, and, adopting the advice contained in the spurious Johnsonian quotation, proceeded to “take a walk in Fleet Street” before settling down for the evening.

===Chinese===
再说，还有那根缺失的手指。当初关于失踪案的原始报道里，全然不曾提及这等残缺，尽管按理它很难被人忽略。可是，没有事实而凭空推想终究无用。这几天里我总要见到桑代克的，毫无疑问，倘若这项发现同约翰·贝林厄姆的失踪有什么牵连，我自会听到消息。怀着这样一番思量，我从桌边起身，依照那句托名约翰逊（Johnson）博士的伪格言所含的劝告，在安顿下来过夜之前，先去“到舰队街（Fleet Street）上散一回步”。

## VI Sidelights / 六 侧闻
===Original===
The association of coal with potatoes is one upon which I have frequently speculated, without arriving at any more satisfactory explanation than that both products are of the earth, earthy. Of the connection itself Barnard’s practise furnished several instances besides Mrs. Jablett’s establishment in Fleur-de-Lys Court, one of which was a dark and mysterious cavern a foot below the level of the street, that burrowed under an ancient house on the west side of Fetter Lane﻿—a crinkly, timber house of the three-decker type that leaned back drunkenly from the road as if about to sit down in its own back yard.

===Chinese===
煤与马铃薯的搭配，是我屡屡加以推究的一件事，却始终不曾得出比这更满意的解释：两种产品都是土里生土里长的土产。至于这种搭配本身，除了贾布利特太太在百合花院的那家铺面，巴纳德的诊业还提供了好几处实例；其中之一，是一个阴暗而神秘的地窖，比街面低下一英尺，钻在菲特巷西边一座老宅底下——那是一座皱巴巴的三层旧木楼，带着醉意从街边朝后仰去，仿佛就要一屁股坐进自家的后院里。

===Original===
Passing this repository of the associated products about ten o’clock in the morning, I perceived in the shadows of the cavern no less a person than Miss Oman. She saw me at the same moment, and beckoned peremptorily with a hand that held a large Spanish onion. I approached with a deferential smile.

===Chinese===
一天早晨十点钟光景，我打这两样联合产品的储藏所门前经过，竟在地窖的暗影里望见一位人物——不是别人，正是奥曼小姐。她也同时瞧见了我，一只握着大个儿西班牙洋葱的手，专断地朝我招了招。我堆起恭恭敬敬的笑脸迎上前去。

===Original===
“What a magnificent onion, Miss Oman! and how generous of you to offer it to me﻿—”

===Chinese===
“好一只漂亮的大洋葱，奥曼小姐！您肯把它送给我，真是太大方了——”

===Original===
“I wasn’t offering it to you. But there! Isn’t it just like a man﻿—”

===Chinese===
“谁要送你来着。可你瞧！男人不就是这个样儿么——”

===Original===
“Isn’t what just like a man?” I interrupted. “If you mean the onion﻿—”

===Chinese===
“什么就是这个样儿？”我插嘴道，“要是您说的是这只洋葱——”

===Original===
“I don’t!” she snapped; “and I wish you wouldn’t talk such a parcel of nonsense. A grown man and a member of a serious profession, too! You ought to know better.”

===Chinese===
“我没说洋葱！”她厉声道，“我也请你别再讲这一大包胡话。堂堂一个大人，还是个正经行当里的人呢！你也该懂点好歹。”

===Original===
“I suppose I ought,” I said reflectively. And she continued:

===Chinese===
“大约是该懂点好歹。”我若有所思地说。她接着道：

===Original===
“I called in at the surgery just now.”

===Chinese===
“我方才到诊所去过。”

===Original===
“To see me?”

===Chinese===
“去看我？”

===Original===
“What else should I come for? Do you suppose that I called to consult the bottle-boy?”

===Chinese===
“不看你还能去看谁？难道你以为，我是去请教那个摆药瓶的小厮的么？”

===Original===
“Certainly not, Miss Oman. So you find the lady doctor no use, after all?”

===Chinese===
“当然不是，奥曼小姐。这么说，到底还是觉得那位女大夫不中用？”

===Original===
Miss Oman gnashed her teeth at me (and very fine teeth they were too).

===Chinese===
奥曼小姐对我咬牙切齿（而且那口牙也着实漂亮）。

===Original===
“I called,” she said majestically, “on behalf of Miss Bellingham.”

===Chinese===
“我去诊所，”她端然庄严地说，“是替贝林厄姆小姐去的。”

===Original===
My facetiousness evaporated instantly. “I hope Miss Bellingham is not ill,” I said with a sudden anxiety that elicited a sardonic smile from Miss Oman.

===Chinese===
我的一脸打趣顿时烟消云散。“但愿贝林厄姆小姐没有生病，”我说；那阵突发的焦灼，惹得奥曼小姐讥诮地一笑。

===Original===
“No,” was the reply, “she is not ill, but she has cut her hand rather badly. It’s her right hand too, and she can’t afford to lose the use of it, not being a great, bulky, lazy, lolloping man. So you had better go and put some stuff on it.”

===Chinese===
“没有，”回答是，“她没生病，可她的手割破了，伤得不轻。偏偏又是右手；她又不是那种高大粗笨、懒懒散散、晃晃悠悠的大男人，这只手可闲废不起。所以你最好赶紧去给上点药。”

===Original===
With this advice, Miss Oman whisked to the right-about and vanished into the depths of the cavern like the witch of Wokey, while I hurried on to the surgery to provide myself with the necessary instruments and materials, and thence proceeded to Nevill’s Court.

===Chinese===
撂下这句忠告，奥曼小姐霍地一个向后转，像乌基（Wokey）洞里的巫婆似的，一头没入地窖深处；我则赶忙奔往诊所，给自己备齐必要的器械材料，随即取道前往内维尔巷。

===Original===
Miss Oman’s juvenile maidservant, who opened the door to me, stated the existing conditions with epigrammatic conciseness.

===Chinese===
开门的是奥曼小姐手下那个半大不小的女仆，她以格言式的简洁说明了眼下的情形。

===Original===
“Mr. Bellingham is hout, sir; but Miss Bellingham is hin.”

===Chinese===
“贝林厄姆先生出去咧，先生；不过贝林厄姆小姐在家咧。”

===Original===
Having thus delivered herself she retreated toward the kitchen and I ascended the stairs, at the head of which I found Miss Bellingham awaiting me with her right hand encased in what looked like a white boxing-glove.

===Chinese===
如此交代完毕，她便退往厨房去了；我登上楼梯，见贝林厄姆小姐正在楼梯口等我，右手裹在一个白色的什么东西里，活像一只拳击手套。

===Original===
“I’m glad you have come,” she said. “Phyllis﻿—Miss Oman, you know﻿—has kindly bound up my hand, but I should like you to see that it is all right.”

===Chinese===
“您来了我很高兴，”她说，“菲莉斯——就是奥曼小姐——好心替我把手包扎上了，不过我想请您瞧瞧，包得妥当不妥当。”

===Original===
We went into the sitting-room, where I laid out my paraphernalia on the table while I inquired into the particulars of the accident.

===Chinese===
我们走进起居室，我把随身的家什摊在桌上，一面细问出事的缘由。

===Original===
“It is most unfortunate that it should have happened just now,” she said, as I wrestled with one of those remarkable feminine knots that, while they seem to defy the utmost efforts of human ingenuity to untie, yet have a singular habit of untying themselves at inopportune moments.

===Chinese===
“偏偏赶上这个当口出事，真是再倒霉不过了，”她说；这时我正跟一个女人家打下的稀奇结子较劲——那种结子，仿佛人间的聪明才智再怎么使尽也解它不开，偏偏又会不合时宜地自己松开。

===Original===
“Why just now in particular?” I asked.

===Chinese===
“为什么偏偏是这个当口？”我问。

===Original===
“Because I have some specially important work to do. A very learned lady who is writing an historical book has commissioned me to collect all the literature relating to the Tell-el-Amarna letters﻿—the cuneiform tablets, you know, of Amenhotep the Fourth.”

===Chinese===
“因为我有一件特别要紧的工作要做。一位正在著一部史书的非常有学问的女士，委托我把有关泰尔-埃尔-阿马尔纳（Tell-el-Amarna）书信的全部文献搜集起来——您知道，就是阿蒙霍特普四世的那批楔形文字泥板。”

===Original===
“Well,” I said soothingly, “I expect your hand will soon be well.”

===Chinese===
“唔，”我宽慰道，“我看您的手很快就会好的。”

===Original===
“Yes, but that won’t do. The work has to be done immediately. I have to send in completed notes not later than this day week, and it will be quite impossible. I am dreadfully disappointed.”

===Chinese===
“是会好的，可那不顶事。这工作得立刻就做。我最迟下星期的今天就得交出完整的笔记，那可就完全办不到了。我失望得不得了。”

===Original===
By this time I had unwound the voluminous wrappings and exposed the injury﻿—a deep gash in the palm that must have narrowly missed a good-sized artery. Obviously the hand would be useless for fully a week.

===Chinese===
这时我已解开那层层叠叠的裹伤布，露出了伤处——掌心里一道深口子，差一丝儿就要伤着一条不小的动脉。显而易见，这只手足足一个星期使唤不得。

===Original===
“I suppose,” she said, “you couldn’t patch it up so that I could write with it?”

===Chinese===
“您看，”她说，“能不能把它拾掇拾掇，好歹让我还能拿它写字？”

===Original===
I shook my head.

===Chinese===
我摇了摇头。

===Original===
“No, Miss Bellingham. I shall have to put it on a splint. We can’t run any risks with a deep wound like this.”

===Chinese===
“不能，贝林厄姆小姐。我得给它上夹板。这样深的伤口，我们可冒不起半点风险。”

===Original===
“Then I shall have to give up the commission, and I don’t know how my client will get the work done in time. You see, I am pretty well up in the literature of Ancient Egypt; in fact, I was to receive special payment on that account. And it would have been such an interesting task, too. However, it can’t be helped.”

===Chinese===
“那我就只好辞掉这桩委托了，也不知道我的主顾怎么来得及把这活儿赶出来。您知道，古埃及的文献我相当熟；老实说，人家也正是为这个才特别加钱的。再说，这本会是一桩多么有趣的工作啊。可是，没法子。”

===Original===
I proceeded methodically with the application of the dressings, and meanwhile reflected. It was evident that she was deeply disappointed. Loss of work meant loss of money, and it needed but a glance at her rusty black dress to see that there was little margin for that. Possibly, too, there was some special need to be met. Her manner seemed almost to imply that there was. And at this point I had a brilliant idea.

===Chinese===
我有条不紊地继续敷药包扎，一面暗自思量。她显然失望得很。误了工作便是误了钱，只消朝她那身褪色的黑衣裙望上一眼，便看得出这里面没有多少余地。说不定还有一笔什么特别的用项等着应付呢。她的神气几乎就暗示着有。恰恰在这当口，我灵机一动。

===Original===
“I’m not sure that it can’t be helped,” said I.

===Chinese===
“也未必就没法子。”我说。

===Original===
She looked at me inquiringly, and I continued: “I am going to make a proposition, and I shall ask you to consider it with an open mind.”

===Chinese===
她带着询问的神色望着我，我便接着说：“我要提一个建议，并且要请您敞开来考虑。”

===Original===
“That sounds rather portentous,” said she; “but I promise. What is it?”

===Chinese===
“这话听着怪郑重其事的，”她说，“不过我答应。什么建议？”

===Original===
“It is this: When I was a student I acquired the useful art of writing shorthand. I am not a lightning reporter, you understand, but I can take matter down from dictation at quite respectable speed.”

===Chinese===
“是这样：我做学生的时候，学会了一门有用的本事——速记。您明白，我不是什么闪电式的访员，不过口授给我、由我记下来，速度还相当过得去。”

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
“嗯。”

===Original===
“Well, I have several hours free every day﻿—usually the whole afternoon up to six or half-past﻿—and it occurs to me that if you were to go to the Museum in the mornings you could get out your books, look up passages (you could do that without using your right hand), and put in bookmarks. Then I could come along in the afternoon and you could read out the selected passages to me, and I could take them down in shorthand. We should get through as much in a couple of hours as you could in a day using longhand.”

===Chinese===
“是这样：我每天有几个钟头的空——通常整个下午到六点或六点半都闲着——我想，您若是上午去博物馆，把书提出来，把段落查好（这不用右手也办得了），再夹上书签；然后我下午过来，您把选定的段落念给我听，我用速记录下来。两个钟头里我们能赶完的，抵得上您用寻常写法写一整天。”

===Original===
“Oh, but how kind of you, Dr. Berkeley!” she exclaimed. “How very kind! Of course, I couldn’t think of taking up all your leisure in that way; but I do appreciate your kindness very much.”

===Chinese===
“哦，您心肠真好，伯克利大夫！”她高声道，“真是太好心了！当然，我怎么也不能让您把闲暇全这么搭进来；不过您的好意，我是万分感激的。”

===Original===
I was rather chapfallen at this very definite refusal, but persisted feebly:

===Chinese===
这般斩钉截铁的谢绝叫我相当垂头丧气，但我还是软软地坚持了一句：

===Original===
“I wish you would. It may seem rather a cheek for a comparative stranger like me to make such a proposal to a lady; but if you’d been a man﻿—in those special circumstances﻿—I should have made it all the same, and you would have accepted as a matter of course.”

===Chinese===
“我但愿您肯。像我这样一个沾亲不带故的人，向一位小姐提出这样的建议，也许显得冒失；可您要是个男人——处在这等特别的境地——我照样会提，而您也会理所当然地接受。”

===Original===
“I doubt that. At any rate, I am not a man. I sometimes wish I were.”

===Chinese===
“那我可怀疑。无论如何，我不是个男人。不过我有时倒希望自己是个男人。”

===Original===
“Oh, I am sure you are much better as you are!” I exclaimed, with such earnestness that we both laughed. And at this moment Mr. Bellingham entered the room carrying several large brand-new books in a strap.

===Chinese===
“哦，我敢说您照现在的样子好得多！”我喊道，那份认真劲儿惹得我们俩都笑了。正在这时，贝林厄姆先生走进屋来，用皮带捆着几部崭新的大书。

===Original===
“Well, I’m sure!” he exclaimed genially; “here are pretty goings on. Doctor and patient giggling like a pair of schoolgirls! What’s the joke?”

===Chinese===
“嚯，真是的！”他和和气气地嚷道，“这儿可有意思了。医生和病人，像一对女学生似的咯咯直笑！有什么乐子呀？”

===Original===
He thumped his parcel of books down on the table and listened smilingly while my unconscious witticism was expounded.

===Chinese===
他把那捆书咚地一声撂在桌上，笑吟吟地听着人讲解我那句无心的妙语。

===Original===
“The doctor’s quite right,” he said. “You’ll do as you are, chick; but the Lord knows what sort of man you would make. You take his advice and let well alone.”

===Chinese===
“医生说得全然不错，”他说，“你照现在的样子就很好，小丫头；可你要做了男人会是个什么款儿，只有天晓得。听他的劝，安于现状、莫再折腾吧。”

===Original===
Finding him in this genial frame of mind, I ventured to explain my proposition to him and to enlist his support. He considered it with attentive approval, and when I had finished turned to his daughter.

===Chinese===
见他有这般好兴致，我便大着胆子把我的建议对他讲了，想争取他的支持。他专心一意、连连称许地听完了，便转向他的女儿。

===Original===
“What is your objection, chick?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“你有什么不乐意的，小丫头？”他问。

===Original===
“It would give Doctor Berkeley such a fearful lot of work,” she answered.

===Chinese===
“这要给伯克利大夫添好大好大一堆活儿。”她答道。

===Original===
“It would give him a fearful lot of pleasure,” I said. “It would really.”

===Chinese===
“这也要给他添好大好大一堆乐子，”我说，“真的。”

===Original===
“Then why not?” said Mr. Bellingham. “We don’t mind being under an obligation to the Doctor, do we?”

===Chinese===
“那还有什么说的？”贝林厄姆先生说，“受医生一个情，咱们不介意吧？”

===Original===
“Oh, it isn’t that!” she exclaimed hastily.

===Chinese===
“哦，不是那个意思！”她急忙叫道。

===Original===
“Then take him at his word. He means it. It is a kind action and he’ll like doing it, I’m sure. That’s all right, Doctor; she accepts, don’t you, chick?”

===Chinese===
“那你就信他的话。他是当真的。这是一桩好心的举动，他准乐意做，我敢保。这就妥了，医生；她答应了——你答应不答应，小丫头？”

===Original===
“Yes, if you say so, I do; and most thankfully.”

===Chinese===
“是，既然你这么说，我就答应；而且是万分感激地答应。”

===Original===
She accompanied the acceptance with a gracious smile that was in itself a large repayment on account, and when we had made the necessary arrangements, I hurried away in a state of the most perfect satisfaction to finish my morning’s work and order an early lunch.

===Chinese===
她允诺时伴以一个温雅的微笑，那微笑本身就算得一笔不小的预付酬金。必要的安排逐一讲定之后，我便怀着万分圆满的心境匆匆离去，赶完上午的事务，又吩咐提早开午饭。

===Original===
When I called for her a couple of hours later I found her waiting in the garden with the shabby handbag, of which I relieved her, and we set forth together, watched jealously by Miss Oman, who had accompanied her to the gate.

===Chinese===
两个钟头后我来接她，见她已在花园里等候，手里拎着那只寒碜的手提包——我把它接了过来；我们便一同出发，奥曼小姐把她送到大门口，一路妒意森森地盯着我们。

===Original===
As I walked up the court with this wonderful maid by my side I could hardly believe in my good fortune. By her presence and my own resulting happiness the mean surroundings became glorified and the commonest objects transfigured into things of beauty. What a delightful thoroughfare, for instance, was Fetter Lane, with its quaint charm and medieval grace! I snuffed the cabbage-laden atmosphere and seemed to breathe the scent of the asphodel. Holborn was even as the Elysian Fields; the omnibus that bore us westward was a chariot of glory; and the people who swarmed verminously on the pavements bore the semblance of the children of light.

===Chinese===
同这位奇妙的小姐并肩走上巷子时，我几乎不敢相信自己的好运。有她在身旁，加上我自己随之而来的幸福，鄙陋的环境全都变得辉煌，最寻常的物件也化成了美的东西。譬如菲特巷，多么可喜的一条通衢，带着它古雅的情致和中世纪的风韵！我吸着满载白菜味的空气，却仿佛吸到了日光兰（asphodel）的芬芳。霍尔本（Holborn）直如极乐野（Elysian Fields）一般；载着我们西去的公共马车是一辆荣耀的战车；人行道上虫豸般糜集的人们，也都俨然光明之子的模样。

===Original===
Love is a foolish thing judged by workaday standards, and the thoughts and actions of lovers foolish beyond measure. But the workaday standard is the wrong one, after all; for the utilitarian mind does but busy itself with the trivial and transitory interests of life, behind which looms the great and everlasting reality of the love of man and woman. There is more significance in a nightingale’s song in the hush of a summer night than in all the wisdom of Solomon (who, by the way, was not without his little experiences of the tender passion).

===Chinese===
拿日常营生的标准来衡量，爱情是一件蠢东西，恋人的心思和举动更蠢得无以复加。可是日常的标准终究是要不得的；因为功利之心所忙碌的，不过是生活中琐屑而短暂的利害，而在这些利害背后，隐然耸立着男女之爱这一伟大而永恒的真实。夏夜岑寂中的一声夜莺啼啭，其意味胜过所罗门的全部智慧（附带说一句，所罗门在柔情蜜意这件事上，也并非没有他自己的一点小阅历）。

===Original===
The janitor in the little glass box by the entrance to the library inspected us and passed us on, with a silent benediction, to the lobby, whence (when I had handed my stick to a bald-headed demigod and received a talismanic disc in exchange) we entered the enormous rotunda of the reading-room.

===Chinese===
图书馆入口处小玻璃匣子里的门房把我们打量了一番，默默祝福着放我们进入前厅；再往前（等我把手杖交给一位秃头的半神、换回一枚符咒似的圆牌之后），我们便走进了阅览室那巨大的圆厅。

===Original===
I have often thought that, if some lethal vapor of highly preservative properties﻿—such as formaldehyde, for instance﻿—could be shed into the atmosphere of this apartment, the entire and complete collection of books and bookworms would be well worth preserving, for the enlightenment of posterity, as a sort of anthropological appendix to the main collection of the Museum. For, surely, nowhere else in the world are so many strange and abnormal human beings gathered together in one place. And a curious question that must have occurred to many observers is: Whence do these singular creatures come, and whither do they go when the very distinct-faced clock (adjusted to literary eyesight) proclaims closing time? The tragic-faced gentleman, for instance, with the corkscrew ringlets that bob up and down like spiral springs as he walks? Or the short, elderly gentleman in the black cassock and bowler hat, who shatters your nerves by turning suddenly and revealing himself as a middle-aged woman? Whither do they go? One never sees them elsewhere. Do they steal away at closing time into the depths of the Museum and hide themselves until morning in sarcophagi or mummy cases? Or do they creep through spaces in the bookshelves and spend the night behind the volumes in a congenial atmosphere of leather and antique paper? Who can say?
